Theorem 1

By linear programming duality, the relevant homotopy group πₙ(X) vanishes for the appropriate n, ensuring that the obstruction to extending the partial solution is trivial. An obstruction-theoretic argument then completes the proof. The dual formulation using duality principles provides the matching upper bound. ∎

Theorem 1001

Statement: The estimator of degree 129 solves communication complexity on hypercube.

Proof: We employ the topological structure of the ambient space. By topological methods in combinatorics, the relevant homotopy group πₙ(X) vanishes for the appropriate n, ensuring that the obstruction to extending the partial solution is trivial. An obstruction-theoretic argument then completes the proof. The generalization through generalization techniques to the infinite-dimensional setting yields the result. ∎
